+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import kotlin.math.floor
+
+internal const val MINUTE = 60
+internal const val HOUR = 3600
+private const val DAY_SECONDS = 86400
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_METRES = 1000000.0
+private const val MAX_PACE_SECONDS = 3600
+private const val MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS = 600
+private const val MIN_PACE_SECONDS = 60
+
+/** Accepts h:mm:ss and m:ss, seconds and minutes 00–59 after a colon; anything else is null. */
+internal fun parseDuration(raw: String): Int? {
+    val hour = Regex("""^(\d+):([0-5]\d):([0-5]\d)$""").matchEntire(raw.trim())
+    if (hour != null) {
+        val parts = hour.groupValues
+        return parts[1].toInt() * HOUR + parts[2].toInt() * MINUTE + parts[3].toInt()
+    }
+    val minute = Regex("""^(\d+):([0-5]\d)$""").matchEntire(raw.trim())
+    if (minute != null) {
+        val parts = minute.groupValues
+        return parts[1].toInt() * MINUTE + parts[2].toInt()
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+/** Pace inputs take m:ss. */
+internal fun parsePace(raw: String): Int? {
+    val match = Regex("""^(\d+):([0-5]\d)$""").matchEntire(raw.trim()) ?: return null
+    return match.groupValues[1].toInt() * MINUTE + match.groupValues[2].toInt()
+}
+
+/** The distance's unit: the rower's distances are metres whatever the workout's unit. */
+internal fun distanceUnitOf(
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): DistanceUnit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+
+/** Whole seconds as the sheet reads them: m:ss under an hour, h:mm:ss from one up. */
+internal fun formatDuration(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+internal fun durationError(raw: String): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val seconds = parseDuration(raw) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+    if (seconds < 1 || seconds > DAY_SECONDS) return "between 1 and $DAY_SECONDS s"
+    return null
+}
+
+internal fun distanceError(
+    raw: String,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val unit = distanceUnitOf(activity, units)
+    val range = "between 0 and ${trimmed(MAX_DISTANCE_METRES / unit.metres)} ${unit.name.lowercase()}"
+    val metres = raw.toDoubleOrNull()?.let { it * unit.metres } ?: return range
+    if (metres <= 0 || metres >= MAX_DISTANCE_METRES) return range
+    return null
+}
+
+internal fun paceError(
+    raw: String,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS else MAX_PACE_SECONDS
+    val seconds = parsePace(raw) ?: return "between $MIN_PACE_SECONDS and $max s"
+    if (seconds < MIN_PACE_SECONDS || seconds > max) return "between $MIN_PACE_SECONDS and $max s"
+    return null
+}
+
+internal fun speedError(
+    raw: String,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val range = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "between 0.1 and 120 mph" else "between 0.1 and 120 km/h"
+    val value = raw.toDoubleOrNull() ?: return range
+    if (value < 0.1 || value > 120.0) return range
+    return null
+}
+
+internal fun maxHeartRateError(
+    raw: String,
+    avgRaw: String,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val value = raw.toIntOrNull() ?: return "between 30 and 250 bpm"
+    if (value < 30 || value > 250) return "between 30 and 250 bpm"
+    val avg = avgRaw.toIntOrNull()
+    if (avg != null && value < avg) return "max heart rate is below average"
+    return null
+}
+
+internal fun cadenceError(
+    raw: String,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val min = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) 10 else 1
+    val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) 60 else 250
+    val unit = if (activity.isRide) "rpm" else "spm"
+    val value = raw.toIntOrNull() ?: return "between $min and $max $unit"
+    if (value < min || value > max) return "between $min and $max $unit"
+    return null
+}
+
+internal fun intInRange(
+    raw: String,
+    min: Int,
+    max: Int,
+    unit: String,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val value = raw.toIntOrNull() ?: return "between $min and $max $unit"
+    if (value < min || value > max) return "between $min and $max $unit"
+    return null
+}
+
+internal fun trimmed(value: Double): String = if (value == floor(value)) value.toLong().toString() else value.toString()
diff --git a/shared/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogDraft.kt b/shared/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogDraft.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8ff5f8e

+++ b/shared/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogDraft.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,175 @@
+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.abs
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's immutable draft (decision `derived-never-sent`): typed [texts] per field plus the
+ * parsed [effort] and the workout's [note]. Pure functions, no Compose; the 400 ms debounce lives in
+ * the sheet (Task 9), not here. A blank pace or speed with both duration and distance shows
+ * [derived] in italics with CALCULATED; the request carries typed fields only.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+    val effort: Int?,
+    val note: String,
+) {
+    /** The typed text, "" when nothing is typed. */
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String = if (field == CardioLogField.NOTE) note else texts[field] ?: ""
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value), effort = value.toIntOrNull())
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    /** Marks the field as left, so its error may show. */
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    /** errors()[field] once the field was left, else null. */
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (left.contains(field)) errors()[field] else null
+
+    /** Every field's error; an empty field is never in error. */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val found = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+        durationError(text(CardioLogField.DURATION))?.let { found[CardioLogField.DURATION] = it }
+        distanceError(text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE), activity, units)?.let { found[CardioLogField.DISTANCE] = it }
+        paceError(text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE), activity)?.let { found[CardioLogField.AVG_PACE] = it }
+        speedError(text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED), units)?.let { found[CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED] = it }
+        elevationError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN] = it }
+        heartRateError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE] = it }
+        maxError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] = it }
+        cadenceError(text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE), activity)?.let { found[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE] = it }
+        intInRange(text(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER), 1, 2500, "W")?.let { found[CardioLogField.AVG_POWER] = it }
+        intInRange(text(CardioLogField.CALORIES), 0, 20000, "kcal")?.let { found[CardioLogField.CALORIES] = it }
+        return found
+    }
+
+    /** The primary is armed only while duration parses and no error stands. */
+    val canSubmit: Boolean
+        get() = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /**
+     * The calculated AVG_PACE ("6:37") or AVG_SPEED ("31.9") when nothing is typed and both duration
+     * and distance are known; null when typed or for any other field.
+     */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        val duration = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION)) ?: return null
+        val distance = typedDistance() ?: return null
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedPace(duration, distance, null, activity, units)?.let { formatDuration(it) }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(duration, distance, null, units)?.let { oneDecimal(it) }
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Typed fields and effort; never the note, never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION)) ?: 0,
+            distance = typedDistance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = parsePace(text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)),
+            avgSpeed = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).toDoubleOrNull(),
+            avgHeartRate = text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull(),
+            maxHeartRate = text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE).toIntOrNull(),
+            calories = text(CardioLogField.CALORIES).toIntOrNull(),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN).toIntOrNull(),
+            avgCadence = text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).toIntOrNull(),
+            avgPower = text(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER).toIntOrNull(),
+        )
+
+    private fun typedDistance(): CardioDistance? {
+        val value = text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).toDoubleOrNull() ?: return null
+        return CardioDistance(value, distanceUnitOf(activity, units))
+    }
+
+    private fun elevationError(): String? {
+        val unit = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"
+        return intInRange(text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN), 0, 20000, unit)
+    }
+
+    private fun heartRateError(): String? = intInRange(text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE), 30, 250, "bpm")
+
+    private fun maxError(): String? = maxHeartRateError(text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE), text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+
+    companion object {
+        /**
+         * A blank draft; when [workout] is in progress its Duration prefills with whole seconds
+         * `now − startedAt` at the moment the sheet opens (decision `clock-from-started-at`).
+         */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val seconds =
+                if (workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS && workout.startedAt != null) {
+                    (now - workout.startedAt).inWholeSeconds
+                } else {
+                    null
+                }
+            val texts =
+                if (seconds != null && seconds >= 0) {
+                    mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to formatDuration(seconds.toInt()))
+                } else {
+                    emptyMap()
+                }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = texts,
+                effort = null,
+                note = workout.notes ?: "",
+            )
+        }
+
+        /** The saved log back as text and effort from it, the note from the workout's notes. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log
+            val texts = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+            if (log != null) {
+                texts[CardioLogField.DURATION] = formatDuration(log.durationSeconds)
+                log.distance?.let { texts[CardioLogField.DISTANCE] = trimmed(it.value) }
+                log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_PACE] = formatDuration(it) }
+                log.avgSpeed?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED] = trimmed(it) }
+                log.elevationGain?.let { texts[CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgHeartRate?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE] = it.toString() }
+                log.maxHeartRate?.let { texts[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgCadence?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgPower?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_POWER] = it.toString() }
+                log.calories?.let { texts[CardioLogField.CALORIES] = it.toString() }
+                log.effort?.let { texts[CardioLogField.EFFORT] = it.toString() }
+            }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = texts,
+                effort = log?.effort,
+                note = workout.notes ?: "",
+            )
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun oneDecimal(value: Double): String {
+    val rounded = (value * 10).roundToLong()
+    return "${rounded / 10}.${abs(rounded % 10)}"
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's field per activity (decision `log-fields-table`), in sheet order — the backend's
+ * `CardioActivity.logFields` sets verbatim. Duration, HR, calories, effort and note ride on every
+ * activity; outdoor runs add distance, pace, elevation and cadence; treadmill drops elevation and
+ * cadence; outdoor rides take speed, elevation, power and cadence; indoor rides drop elevation;
+ * the rower takes distance, pace, power and cadence; elliptical takes distance and cadence.
+ */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val RunFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+private val OutdoorRideFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+private val RowerFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+/** The fields the log sheet draws for [this] activity, in sheet order. */
+val CardioActivity.logFields: List<CardioLogField>
+    get() =
+        when (this) {
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+                RunFields
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+                RunFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN - CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                OutdoorRideFields
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                OutdoorRideFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+                RowerFields
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+        }

+class CardioLogDraftTest {

+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity,

+        startedAt: Instant? = null,

+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),

+    ) = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(activity, units))

+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }

+    @Test

+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", errors[CardioLogField.DURATION])

+        val filled =
+            draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", filled.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(filled.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val filled =
+            draft(CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "12:36")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "3000")
+        assertEquals("2:06", filled.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(3000.0, DistanceUnit.M), filled.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val filled =
+            draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", filled.errors()[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE])
+        assertNull(filled.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE])
+        assertFalse(filled.canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val errors =
+            draft(CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "12:36")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+                .errors()
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", errors[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val filled =
+            draft(CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "12:36")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertTrue(filled.errors().containsKey(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertNull(filled.shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", filled.left(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val started = Instant.parse("2026-10-27T18:31:00.000Z")
+        val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-27T19:12:06.900Z")
+        val fresh =
+            CardioLogDraft.fresh(
+                workout(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = started),
+                now,
+            )
+        assertEquals("41:06", fresh.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val saved =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 1992,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgPaceSeconds = 397,
+                avgHeartRate = 148,
+                maxHeartRate = 162,
+                calories = 320,
+                effort = 6,
+                elevationGain = 24,
+                avgCadence = 178,
+            )
+        val filled = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, log = saved, notes = "hilly"))
+        assertEquals("33:12", filled.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", filled.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("6:37", filled.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("148", filled.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("162", filled.text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("320", filled.text(CardioLogField.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("24", filled.text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("178", filled.text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals(6, filled.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", filled.note)
+        assertEquals("hilly", filled.text(CardioLogField.NOTE))
+        assertTrue(filled.errors().isEmpty())
+        assertTrue(filled.canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val logOnlyDerived = CardioLog(durationSeconds = 4360, distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM))
+        val derivedOnly = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, log = logOnlyDerived))
+        assertEquals("", derivedOnly.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", derivedOnly.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+
+        val typed =
+            draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(CardioLogField.CALORIES, "320")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        val request = typed.toRequest()
+        assertEquals(1992, request.durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM), request.distance)
+        assertEquals(148, request.avgHeartRate)
+        assertEquals(320, request.calories)
+        assertEquals(6, request.effort)
+        assertNull(request.avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertNull(request.avgSpeed)
+
+        val rower =
+            draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, DistanceUnit.KM)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "23:52")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), rower.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.floor
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val MAX_DURATION_SECONDS = 86400
+private const val MIN_HEART_RATE = 30
+private const val MAX_HEART_RATE = 250
+private const val MAX_CALORIES = 20000
+private const val MAX_ELEVATION = 20000
+private const val MIN_PACE_SECONDS = 60
+private const val MAX_PACE_SECONDS = 3600
+private const val MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS = 600
+private const val MIN_SPEED = 0.1
+private const val MAX_SPEED = 120.0
+private const val MIN_CADENCE = 1
+private const val MAX_CADENCE = 250
+private const val MIN_ROWER_CADENCE = 10
+private const val MAX_ROWER_CADENCE = 60
+private const val MIN_POWER = 1
+private const val MAX_POWER = 2500
+private const val MIN_EFFORT = 1
+private const val MAX_EFFORT = 10
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_METRES = 1000000.0
+private const val SECONDS_PER_MINUTE = 60
+private const val SECONDS_PER_HOUR = 3600
+private const val MI_MAX_DISTANCE = 621.37
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's typed values (KAN-939 step 4, decisions `duration-input`, `client-ranges`,
+ * `derived-never-sent`, `clock-from-started-at`): an immutable data class with pure functions, no
+ * Compose. [texts] holds the raw typed text per field; [effort] and [note] are properties so
+ * `copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")` works. The 400 ms debounce lives in the sheet, not here.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+    val distanceUnit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    /** The typed text, or "" when nothing is typed. */
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString() ?: ""
+            else -> texts[field] ?: ""
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toIntOrNull())
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    /** Marks the field as left, so [shownError] starts reporting it. */
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    /** `errors()[field]` once the field was left, else null. */
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    /** Field errors for every typed value; an empty field is never in error. */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val found = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+        durationError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.DURATION] = it }
+        distanceError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.DISTANCE] = it }
+        paceError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.AVG_PACE] = it }
+        speedError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED] = it }
+        intFieldError(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, 0, MAX_ELEVATION, elevationUnit())?.let {
+            found[CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN] = it
+        }
+        intFieldError(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, MIN_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, "bpm")?.let {
+            found[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE] = it
+        }
+        heartMaxError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] = it }
+        cadenceError()?.let { found[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE] = it }
+        intFieldError(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, MIN_POWER, MAX_POWER, "W")?.let {
+            found[CardioLogField.AVG_POWER] = it
+        }
+        intFieldError(CardioLogField.CALORIES, 0, MAX_CALORIES, "kcal")?.let {
+            found[CardioLogField.CALORIES] = it
+        }
+        if (effort != null && (effort < MIN_EFFORT || effort > MAX_EFFORT)) {
+            found[CardioLogField.EFFORT] = "between $MIN_EFFORT and $MAX_EFFORT"
+        }
+        return found
+    }
+
+    /** The primary is enabled: duration parses and nothing is in error. */
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /**
+     * The derived pace ("6:37") or speed ("31.9") while its field is blank — null once typed, and
+     * never sent by [toRequest].
+     */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val seconds = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION)) ?: return null
+        val distance = parsedDistance() ?: return null
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE ->
+                if (text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE).isBlank()) {
+                    CardioFormat.derivedPace(seconds, distance, null, activity, distanceUnit)?.let { formatClock(it.toLong()) }
+                } else {
+                    null
+                }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED ->
+                if (text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).isBlank()) {
+                    CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(seconds, distance, null, distanceUnit)?.let(::formatOneDecimal)
+                } else {
+                    null
+                }
+
+            else -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Typed fields and effort only — never the note, never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest {
+        check(canSubmit) { "a cardio log draft is not submittable" }
+        val distanceValue = text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).trim().toDouble()
+        return CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION))!!,
+            distance =
+                if (text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).isBlank()) {
+                    null
+                } else if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+                    CardioDistance(distanceValue, DistanceUnit.M)
+                } else {
+                    CardioDistance(distanceValue, distanceUnit)
+                },
+            avgPaceSeconds = text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE).trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let(::parseClock),
+            avgSpeed = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toDouble(),
+            avgHeartRate = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = intOrNull(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = intOrNull(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = intOrNull(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+        )
+    }
+
+    private fun durationError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.DURATION).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val seconds = parseDuration(raw) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+        if (seconds < 1 || seconds > MAX_DURATION_SECONDS) return "between 1 and $MAX_DURATION_SECONDS s"
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val value = raw.toDoubleOrNull() ?: return distanceRangeMessage()
+        val metres = value * distanceMetres()
+        if (metres <= 0 || metres >= MAX_DISTANCE_METRES) return distanceRangeMessage()
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceMetres(): Double = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) 1.0 else distanceUnit.metres
+
+    private fun distanceRangeMessage(): String =
+        when {
+            activity == CardioActivity.ROWER -> "between 0 and ${MAX_DISTANCE_METRES.toLong()} m"
+            distanceUnit == DistanceUnit.MI -> "between 0 and $MI_MAX_DISTANCE mi"
+            else -> "between 0 and ${(MAX_DISTANCE_METRES / DistanceUnit.KM.metres).toLong()} km"
+        }
+
+    private fun paceError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val seconds = parsePace(raw) ?: return "use m:ss"
+        val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS else MAX_PACE_SECONDS
+        if (seconds < MIN_PACE_SECONDS || seconds > max) return paceRangeMessage(max)
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun paceRangeMessage(max: Int): String {
+        val unit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) "/500 m" else "/${distanceUnit.name.lowercase()}"
+        return "between ${formatClock(MIN_PACE_SECONDS.toLong())} and ${formatClock(max.toLong())} $unit"
+    }
+
+    private fun speedError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val value = raw.toDoubleOrNull()
+        val unit = if (distanceUnit == DistanceUnit.MI) "mph" else "km/h"
+        if (value == null || value < MIN_SPEED || value > MAX_SPEED) return "between $MIN_SPEED and $MAX_SPEED $unit"
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun elevationUnit(): String = if (distanceUnit == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"
+
+    private fun intFieldError(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        min: Int,
+        max: Int,
+        unit: String,
+    ): String? {
+        val raw = text(field).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val value = raw.toIntOrNull()
+        if (value == null || value < min || value > max) return "between $min and $max $unit"
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun heartMaxError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val max = raw.toIntOrNull()
+        if (max == null || max < MIN_HEART_RATE || max > MAX_HEART_RATE) return "between $MIN_HEART_RATE and $MAX_HEART_RATE bpm"
+        val avg = text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toIntOrNull()
+        if (avg != null && max < avg) return "max heart rate is below average"
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun cadenceError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val value = raw.toIntOrNull()
+        val min = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MIN_ROWER_CADENCE else MIN_CADENCE
+        val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_CADENCE else MAX_CADENCE
+        val unit = if (activity.isRide) "rpm" else "spm"
+        if (value == null || value < min || value > max) return "between $min and $max $unit"
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun intOrNull(field: CardioLogField): Int? =
+        text(field).trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toInt()
+
+    private fun parsedDistance(): CardioDistance? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val value = raw.toDoubleOrNull() ?: return null
+        if (value <= 0) return null
+        return if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) CardioDistance(value, DistanceUnit.M) else CardioDistance(value, distanceUnit)
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        /** A fresh draft: duration prefilled from the clock (floored to the second) when in progress. */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val seconds =
+                if (workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS && workout.startedAt != null) {
+                    (now - workout.startedAt).inWholeSeconds
+                } else {
+                    0
+                }
+            val duration = if (seconds > 0) formatClock(seconds) else ""
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                distanceUnit = workout.distanceUnit,
+                note = workout.notes ?: "",
+                texts = if (duration.isEmpty()) emptyMap() else mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to duration),
+            )
+        }
+
+        /** A saved log back as text — typed values only, so a derived pace recomputes; note from the workout. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log
+            val texts = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+            if (log != null) {
+                texts[CardioLogField.DURATION] = formatClock(log.durationSeconds.toLong())
+                log.distance?.let { texts[CardioLogField.DISTANCE] = formatDistance(it, workout) }
+                log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_PACE] = formatClock(it.toLong()) }
+                log.avgSpeed?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED] = formatNumber(it) }
+                log.elevationGain?.let { texts[CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgHeartRate?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE] = it.toString() }
+                log.maxHeartRate?.let { texts[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgCadence?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgPower?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_POWER] = it.toString() }
+                log.calories?.let { texts[CardioLogField.CALORIES] = it.toString() }
+            }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                distanceUnit = workout.distanceUnit,
+                effort = log?.effort,
+                note = workout.notes ?: "",
+                texts = texts,
+            )
+        }
+
+        private fun formatDistance(
+            distance: CardioDistance,
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+        ): String =
+            if (workout.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+                (distance.value * distance.unit.metres).roundToLong().toString()
+            } else {
+                formatNumber(distance.value * distance.unit.metres / workout.distanceUnit.metres)
+            }
+    }
+}
+
+/** `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up. */
+private fun formatClock(seconds: Long): String =
+    if (seconds < SECONDS_PER_HOUR) {
+        clock(seconds.toInt())
+    } else {
+        hourClock(seconds.toInt())
+    }
+
+/** `h:mm:ss` and `m:ss` with seconds and minutes 00-59 after a colon, whole seconds — else null. */
+private fun parseDuration(raw: String): Int? {
+    val parts = raw.trim().split(":")
+    val numbers = parts.map { it.toIntOrNull() ?: return null }
+    return when (parts.size) {
+        2 -> {
+            val minute = numbers[0]
+            val second = numbers[1]
+            if (parts[0].isEmpty() || !twoDigits(parts[1]) || second > 59) return null
+            minute * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + second
+        }
+
+        3 -> {
+            val hour = numbers[0]
+            val minute = numbers[1]
+            val second = numbers[2]
+            if (parts[0].isEmpty() || !twoDigits(parts[1]) || !twoDigits(parts[2])) return null
+            if (minute > 59 || second > 59) return null
+            hour * SECONDS_PER_HOUR + minute * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + second
+        }
+
+        else -> null
+    }
+}
+
+private fun twoDigits(part: String): Boolean = part.length in 1..2 && part.all { it.isDigit() }
+
+/** Pace inputs take `m:ss`. */
+private fun parsePace(raw: String): Int? {
+    val parts = raw.trim().split(":")
+    if (parts.size != 2 || parts[0].isEmpty() || !twoDigits(parts[1])) return null
+    val minute = parts[0].toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+    val second = parts[1].toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+    if (second > 59) return null
+    return minute * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + second
+}
+
+private fun parseClock(raw: String): Int = parseDuration(raw) ?: parsePace(raw) ?: 0
+
+private fun formatNumber(value: Double): String = if (value == floor(value)) value.toLong().toString() else value.toString()
+
+private fun formatOneDecimal(value: Double): String {
+    val scaled = (value * 10).roundToLong()
+    val whole = scaled / 10
+    val tenth = (scaled % 10).toString().last()
+    return "$whole.$tenth"
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/**
+ * One field of the log sheet (KAN-939 step 4, decision `log-fields-table`): the frontend table
+ * mirrors the backend's `CardioActivity.logFields` sets, in the frames' sheet order.
+ */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+/** The sheet's fields for this activity, in sheet order — duration, HR, calories, effort and note on every activity. */
+val CardioActivity.logFields: List<CardioLogField>
+    get() =
+        when (this) {
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN ->
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE ->
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE ->
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+
+            CardioActivity.ROWER ->
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN ->
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields -
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL ->
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+        }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio

+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus

+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue

+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private val km = DistanceUnit.KM
+
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        units: DistanceUnit = km,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 20),
+        displayName = "Morning run",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = CardioKind.EASY,
+        distanceUnit = units,
+        log = log,
+    )

+    private fun typed(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        units: DistanceUnit = km,
+        vararg fields: Pair<CardioLogField, String>,
+    ): CardioLogDraft {
+        var draft = CardioLogDraft(activity = activity, distanceUnit = units)
+        fields.forEach { (field, value) -> draft = draft.with(field, value) }
+        return draft
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        assertTrue(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.kinds.isNotEmpty())
+        assertEquals(

+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields,

+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN - CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,

+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,

+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields,
+        )
+    }

+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, typed(CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, typed(CardioLogField.DURATION to "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach { shape ->
+            val draft = typed(CardioLogField.DURATION to shape)
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.DURATION], shape)
+            assertFalse(draft.canSubmit, shape)
+        }
+    }

+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-26T19:12:06.900Z"))
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertFalse(draft.canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val draft =

+            )
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))

+        assertTrue(draft.canSubmit)
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }

+    @Test

+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "5.02",
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE to "6:45",
+            )
+        assertNull(draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(405, draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)

+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(

+                *arrayOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION to "1:12:40",
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "38.6",

+            )
+        assertEquals("31.9", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgSpeed)

+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                activity = CardioActivity.ROWER,
+                *arrayOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION to "8:24",
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "2000",
+                ),
+            )
+        assertEquals("2:06", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(

+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "148",
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to "139",
+            )
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE])
+        assertNull(draft.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE])
+        assertFalse(draft.canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                activity = CardioActivity.ROWER,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to "8",
+            )
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val draft = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to "8")
+        assertEquals("between 1 and 250 spm", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+        assertNull(draft.shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("between 1 and 250 spm", draft.left(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft.fresh(
+                workout(
+                    status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS,
+                    startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-26T18:31:00Z"),
+                ),
+                Instant.parse("2026-10-26T19:12:06.900Z"),
+            )
+        assertEquals("41:06", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft.of(
+                workout(
+                    notes = "hilly",
+                    log =
+                        CardioLog(
+                            durationSeconds = 4360,
+                            distance = CardioDistance(5.02, km),
+                            avgHeartRate = 148,
+                            maxHeartRate = 155,
+                            calories = 420,
+                            effort = 6,
+                            elevationGain = 120,
+                            avgCadence = 172,
+                            derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 868,
+                        ),
+                ),
+            )
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", draft.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("148", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("155", draft.text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("420", draft.text(CardioLogField.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("120", draft.text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("172", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("hilly", draft.note)
+        assertEquals(6, draft.copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly").effort)
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertTrue(draft.canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "5.02",
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "148",
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT to "6",
+                CardioLogField.NOTE to "hilly",
+            )
+        assertTrue(draft.canSubmit)
+        val request = draft.toRequest()
+        assertEquals(1992, request.durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5.02, km), request.distance)
+        assertEquals(148, request.avgHeartRate)
+        assertEquals(6, request.effort)
+        assertNull(request.avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertNull(request.avgSpeed)
+        assertNull(request.maxHeartRate)
+    }
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val MIN_PACE_SECONDS = 60
+private const val MAX_PACE_SECONDS = 3600
+private const val MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS = 600
+private const val MAX_DURATION_SECONDS = 86400
+private const val MIN_HEART_RATE = 30
+private const val MAX_HEART_RATE = 250
+private const val MAX_CALORIES = 20000
+private const val MAX_ELEVATION = 20000
+private const val MIN_SPEED = 0.1
+private const val MAX_SPEED = 120.0
+private const val MIN_CADENCE = 1
+private const val MAX_CADENCE = 250
+private const val MIN_ROWER_CADENCE = 10
+private const val MAX_ROWER_CADENCE = 60
+private const val MIN_POWER = 1
+private const val MAX_POWER = 2500
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_METRES = 1000000.0
+private const val MIN_EFFORT = 1
+private const val MAX_EFFORT = 10
+private const val SECONDS_PER_MINUTE = 60L
+private const val SECONDS_PER_HOUR = 3600L
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's state (decision `log-fields-table`, `duration-input`, `client-ranges`,
+ * `derived-never-sent`): typed [texts] per field with [effort] and [note] as properties, the
+ * [left] fields whose errors may show, and pure derivation of pace/speed. The 400 ms debounce
+ * lives in the sheet, not here.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    /** What the field shows; "" when nothing is typed. */
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> texts[field] ?: effort?.toString() ?: ""
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field] ?: ""
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> {
+                copy(
+                    effort = value.trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toIntOrNull(),
+                    texts = texts + (field to value),
+                )
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+                copy(note = value)
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+            }
+        }
+
+    /** Marks the field as left, so its error may show. */
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    /** errors()[field] once the field was left, else null. */
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    /** Every typed field's range or shape error; an empty field is never in error. */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> =
+        activity.logFields
+            .mapNotNull { field ->
+                errorFor(field)?.let { field to it }
+            }.toMap()
+
+    /** The primary may go: duration parses and nothing typed is out of range. */
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = parseDurationSeconds(text(CardioLogField.DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /**
+     * The sheet's CALCULATED line: pace as "6:37", speed with one decimal as "31.9" — only when
+     * the field is blank and duration and distance are known, never when typed.
+     */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val durationSeconds = parseDurationSeconds(text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        val distance = parsedDistance()
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                val typed = text(field).ifBlank { null }?.let { parsePaceSeconds(it) }
+                CardioFormat
+                    .derivedPace(durationSeconds, distance, typed, activity, units)
+                    ?.let { formatClock(it.toLong()) }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                val typed = text(field).ifBlank { null }?.let { it.trim().toDoubleOrNull() }
+                CardioFormat
+                    .derivedSpeed(durationSeconds, distance, typed, units)
+                    ?.toString()
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The log call's body: typed fields and effort — never the note, never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = parseDurationSeconds(text(CardioLogField.DURATION)) ?: 0,
+            distance = parsedDistance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE).ifBlank { null }?.let { parsePaceSeconds(it) },
+            avgSpeed = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).ifBlank { null }?.let { it.trim().toDoubleOrNull() },
+            avgHeartRate = text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).ifBlank { null }?.toIntOrNull(),
+            maxHeartRate = text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE).ifBlank { null }?.toIntOrNull(),
+            calories = text(CardioLogField.CALORIES).ifBlank { null }?.toIntOrNull(),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN).ifBlank { null }?.toIntOrNull(),
+            avgCadence = text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).ifBlank { null }?.toIntOrNull(),
+            avgPower = text(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER).ifBlank { null }?.toIntOrNull(),
+        )
+
+    private fun errorFor(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val raw = text(field)
+        if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.DURATION -> {
+                durationError(raw)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> {
+                distanceError(raw)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                paceError(raw)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                speedError(raw)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> {
+                intError(raw, 0, MAX_ELEVATION, elevationUnit())
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE -> {
+                intError(raw, MIN_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, "bpm")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+                maxHeartRateError(raw)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> {
+                cadenceError(raw)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> {
+                intError(raw, MIN_POWER, MAX_POWER, "W")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.CALORIES -> {
+                intError(raw, 0, MAX_CALORIES, "kcal")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> {
+                if (effort == null ||
+                    effort !in MIN_EFFORT..MAX_EFFORT
+                ) {
+                    "between $MIN_EFFORT and $MAX_EFFORT"
+                } else {
+                    null
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun durationError(raw: String): String? {
+        val seconds = parseDurationSeconds(raw) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+        return if (seconds !in 1..MAX_DURATION_SECONDS) "between 1 and $MAX_DURATION_SECONDS s" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceError(raw: String): String? {
+        val value = raw.trim().toDoubleOrNull() ?: return "use a number"
+        val metres = value * distanceUnit().metres
+        return if (metres <= 0 || metres >= MAX_DISTANCE_METRES) distanceRange() else null
+    }
+
+    private fun paceError(raw: String): String? {
+        val seconds = parsePaceSeconds(raw) ?: return "use m:ss"
+        val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS else MAX_PACE_SECONDS
+        if (seconds !in MIN_PACE_SECONDS..max) {
+            val unit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) "/500 m" else "/${units.name.lowercase()}"
+            return "between ${formatClock(MIN_PACE_SECONDS.toLong())} and ${formatClock(max.toLong())} $unit"
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun speedError(raw: String): String? {
+        val value = raw.trim().toDoubleOrNull() ?: return "use a number"
+        if (value < MIN_SPEED || value > MAX_SPEED) {
+            val unit = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "mph" else "km/h"
+            return "between $MIN_SPEED and $MAX_SPEED $unit"
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun maxHeartRateError(raw: String): String? {
+        val value = raw.trim().toIntOrNull() ?: return "use a whole number"
+        if (value !in MIN_HEART_RATE..MAX_HEART_RATE) return "between $MIN_HEART_RATE and $MAX_HEART_RATE bpm"
+        val avg = text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).ifBlank { null }?.toIntOrNull()
+        if (avg != null && value < avg) return "max heart rate is below average"
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun cadenceError(raw: String): String? {
+        val value = raw.trim().toIntOrNull() ?: return "use a whole number"
+        val min = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MIN_ROWER_CADENCE else MIN_CADENCE
+        val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_CADENCE else MAX_CADENCE
+        if (value !in min..max) {
+            val unit = if (activity.isRide) "rpm" else "spm"
+            return "between $min and $max $unit"
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun intError(
+        raw: String,
+        min: Int,
+        max: Int,
+        unit: String,
+    ): String? {
+        val value = raw.trim().toIntOrNull() ?: return "use a whole number"
+        return if (value !in min..max) "between $min and $max $unit" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun elevationUnit(): String = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"
+
+    /** The typed distance in the request's unit — metres whatever the workout's unit on a rower. */
+    private fun parsedDistance(): CardioDistance? {
+        val value = texts[CardioLogField.DISTANCE]?.trim()?.ifEmpty { null }?.toDoubleOrNull() ?: return null
+        return CardioDistance(value, distanceUnit())
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceUnit(): DistanceUnit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+
+    private fun distanceRange(): String =
+        if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+            "between 0 and ${MAX_DISTANCE_METRES.toLong()} m"
+        } else {
+            "between 0 and 1000 ${units.name.lowercase()}"
+        }
+
+    companion object {
+        /**
+         * A blank sheet for [workout] at [now] (decision `clock-from-started-at`): duration
+         * prefills with whole seconds since `startedAt` while in progress, floored to the second.
+         */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val draft = CardioLogDraft(activity = workout.activity, units = workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val startedAt = workout.startedAt
+            if (workout.status != WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS || startedAt == null) return draft
+            val seconds = (now - startedAt).inWholeSeconds
+            if (seconds <= 0) return draft
+            return draft.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, formatClock(seconds))
+        }
+
+        /** The saved log back as text — typed values only, so a derived pace recomputes. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log
+            var draft = CardioLogDraft(activity = workout.activity, units = workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            if (log == null) return draft
+            draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, formatClock(log.durationSeconds.toLong()))
+            log.distance?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, distanceText(workout.activity, it)) }
+            log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, formatClock(it.toLong())) }
+            log.avgSpeed?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, it.toString()) }
+            log.elevationGain?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, it.toString()) }
+            log.avgHeartRate?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+            log.maxHeartRate?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+            log.avgCadence?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, it.toString()) }
+            log.avgPower?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, it.toString()) }
+            log.calories?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.CALORIES, it.toString()) }
+            return draft.copy(effort = log.effort)
+        }
+
+        private fun distanceText(
+            activity: CardioActivity,
+            distance: CardioDistance,
+        ): String =
+            if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+                (distance.value * distance.unit.metres).roundToLong().toString()
+            } else {
+                distance.value.toString()
+            }
+    }
+}
+
+/** `h:mm:ss` and `m:ss`, whole seconds; seconds and minutes 00–59 after a colon. */
+internal fun parseDurationSeconds(text: String): Int? {
+    val parts = text.trim().split(":").map { it.toIntOrNull() ?: return null }
+    if (parts.any { it < 0 }) return null
+    return when (parts.size) {
+        2 -> {
+            val (minutes, seconds) = parts
+            if (seconds > 59) return null
+            minutes * 60 + seconds
+        }
+
+        3 -> {
+            val (hours, minutes, seconds) = parts
+            if (minutes > 59 || seconds > 59) return null
+            hours * 3600 + minutes * 60 + seconds
+        }
+
+        else -> {
+            null
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/** Pace inputs take `m:ss`. */
+internal fun parsePaceSeconds(text: String): Int? {
+    val parts = text.trim().split(":").map { it.toIntOrNull() ?: return null }
+    if (parts.size != 2 || parts.any { it < 0 } || parts[1] > 59) return null
+    return parts[0] * 60 + parts[1]
+}
+
+/** Duration text: `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up. */
+internal fun formatClock(totalSeconds: Long): String =
+    if (totalSeconds < SECONDS_PER_HOUR) {
+        "${totalSeconds / SECONDS_PER_MINUTE}:${(totalSeconds % SECONDS_PER_MINUTE).toString().padStart(2, '0')}"
+    } else {
+        "${totalSeconds / SECONDS_PER_HOUR}:" +
+            (totalSeconds % SECONDS_PER_HOUR / SECONDS_PER_MINUTE).toString().padStart(2, '0') + ":" +
+            (totalSeconds % SECONDS_PER_MINUTE).toString().padStart(2, '0')
+    }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/**
+ * One row of the log sheet, in sheet order (decision `log-fields-table`); the table mirrors the
+ * backend's `CardioActivity.logFields` sets. Treadmill, indoor ride and elliptical are built from
+ * the drawn three: the treadmill is the outdoor run's minus elevation and cadence, the indoor ride
+ * the outdoor ride's minus elevation.
+ */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val outdoorRunFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+private val outdoorRideFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+/** The sheet's field set for this activity, in the order the frames draw it (L1, L3, L4). */
+val CardioActivity.logFields: List<CardioLogField>
+    get() =
+        when (this) {
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+                outdoorRunFields
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+                outdoorRunFields.filterNot {
+                    it == CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN || it == CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                outdoorRideFields
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                outdoorRideFields.filterNot { it == CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN }
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+        }

+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft.of(
+                workout(
+                    CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+                    log =
+                        CardioLog(
+                            durationSeconds = 1992,
+                            distance = CardioDistance(5.02, km),
+                            avgPaceSeconds = 397,
+                            avgHeartRate = 148,
+                            maxHeartRate = 152,
+                            avgCadence = 172,
+                            calories = 320,
+                            effort = 6,
+                            elevationGain = 24,
+                        ),
+                    notes = "hilly",
+                ),
+            )
+        assertEquals("33:12", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", draft.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("6:37", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("148", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("152", draft.text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("172", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("320", draft.text(CardioLogField.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("24", draft.text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals(6, draft.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", draft.note)
+        assertNull(draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+
+        val derivedOnly =
+            CardioLogDraft.of(
+                workout(
+                    CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+                    log =
+                        CardioLog(
+                            durationSeconds = 4360,
+                            distance = CardioDistance(5.02, km),
+                            derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+                        ),
+                ),
+            )
+        assertEquals("", derivedOnly.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", derivedOnly.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val draft =
+            freshRun()
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "152")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "172")
+                .with(CardioLogField.CALORIES, "320")
+                .with(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, "24")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        val request = draft.toRequest()
+        assertEquals(1992, request.durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5.02, km), request.distance)
+        assertEquals(148, request.avgHeartRate)
+        assertEquals(152, request.maxHeartRate)
+        assertEquals(172, request.avgCadence)
+        assertEquals(320, request.calories)
+        assertEquals(24, request.elevationGain)
+        assertEquals(6, request.effort)
+        assertNull(request.avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertNull(request.avgSpeed)
+        assertEquals(true, draft.canSubmit)
+        assertEquals("use a number", draft.with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "far").errors()[CardioLogField.DISTANCE])
+        assertEquals("between 1 and 10", draft.copy(effort = 11).errors()[CardioLogField.EFFORT])
+    }
+}
